נקרצת – it is cut and severed and its example we taught in Talmud Yoma (31b): “he cut [the windpipe and the gullet] and another priest completed the slaughtering on his behalf” and in the Bible (Jeremiah 46:20): “a butcher from the north is coming.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:7)


**גַּרְגֵּר יְחִידִי.** כְּגוֹן אֶשְׁכּוֹל שֶׁאֵין לוֹ אֶשְׁכּוֹלוֹת קְטַנּוֹת שׁוֹכְבוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ אֶלָּא הַגַּרְגְּרִים מְחֻבָּרִים בַּשַּׁרְבִיט עַצְמוֹ:


גרגר יחידי – such as a cluster that lacks small clusters lying one on top of the other but the single berries (not growing in clusters) are attached to the shoot/twig itself.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:8)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אֶשְׁכּוֹל.** דִּכְתִיב (ישעיה יז) וְנִשְׁאַר בּוֹ עוֹלְלֹת כְּנֹקֵף זַיִת שְׁנַיִם שְׁלֹשָׁה גַּרְגְּרִים. שְׁנַיִם שְׁלֹשָׁה גַּרְגְּרִים עוֹלְלוֹת, יוֹתֵר מִכָּאן אֶשְׁכּוֹל:


רבי יהודה אומר אשכול – as it is written (Isaiah 17:6): “Only gleanings shall be left of him, as when one beats an olive tree, two or three berries…” are a small single bunch, more than this is a cluster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:4:9)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים עוֹלֶלֶת.** דְּלֹא חֲשִׁיבֵי הַגַּרְגְּרִים הַשּׁוֹכְבִים זֶה עַל זֶה לְהִקָּרֵא כָּתֵף. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים עוללת – and they are not considered berries that lie one on top of the other to be called a shoulder (grapes on an arm of a vine which branches off into twigs). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:1)


**הַמֵּדֵל בַּגְּפָנִים.** כְּשֶׁהַגְּפָנִים תְּכוּפִים זֶה אַחַר זֶה עוֹקֵר מֵאוֹתָם שֶׁבֵּינְתַּיִם וְהָאֲחֵרִים מְתֻקָּנִים בְּכָךְ:


המדל בגפנים – when the vines are joined in close contact one after another, he uproots from those that are in-between and the others are fixed with this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:2)


**כָּךְ הוּא מֵדֵל בְּשֶׁל עֲנִיִּים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶם פֵּאָה אוֹ עוֹלְלוֹת שֶׁל עֲנִיִּים, כָּךְ מֵדֵל בְּשֶׁלָּהֶם כְּמוֹ בְשֶׁלּוֹ, קָסָבַר עֲנִיִּים דִּין שֻׁתָּף יֵשׁ לָהֶם, וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁשֻּׁתָּף כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מֵדֵל בְּשֶׁלּוֹ כָּךְ מֵדֵל בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ אַף עֲנִיִּים כֵּן:


כך הוא מדל בשל עניים – even though they have Peah or a small single bunch (on a single branch – or hanging down from the trunk)/gleaning reserved for the poor so the thinning of theirs like with his, for he holds that they have a partnership law and just as he thins his own, so too he thins that [the vines] of his friends and even the poor are included.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:5:3)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר בְּשֶׁלּוֹ הוּא רַשַּׁאי.** קָסָבַר עֲנִיִּים דִּין קוֹנֶה יֵשׁ לָהֶם בְּחֶלְקָם, וּכְשֵׁם שֶׁהַמּוֹכֵר לַחֲבֵרוֹ עֲשָׂרָה אֶשְׁכּוֹלוֹת אָסוּר לִגַּע בָּהֶן אַף בְּשֶׁל עֲנִיִּים כֵּן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי מאיר אומר בשלו הוא רשאי – He holds that the poor have a law of acquisition with their part, and just as the seller says to his fellow – ten clusters of grapes it is prohibited to touch them, so too regarding that of the poor. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:1)


**כֶּרֶם רְבָעִי.** הַנּוֹטֵעַ כָּל עֵץ מַאֲכָל, בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית מַעֲלֶה הַפֵּרוֹת לִירוּשָׁלַיִם וְאוֹכְלָם שָׁם בִּקְדֻשַּׁת מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי, אוֹ פּוֹדֶה אוֹתָם וּמַעֲלֶה הַדָּמִים לִירוּשָׁלַיִם, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יט) וּבַשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית יִהְיֶה כָּל פִּרְיוֹ קֹדֶשׁ הִלּוּלִים, וְדָרְשִׁינַן הִלּוּלִים כְּמוֹ חִלוּלִים, אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא אַחֲלֵיהּ וְהָדָר אַכְלֵיהּ:


כרם רבעי – A person who plants a food-bearing tree, in the fourth year, he brings the fruit to Jerusalem and eats them there in the holiness of the Second Tithe, or redeems them and brings their monetary-value to Jerusalem, as it is written (Leviticus 19:24): “In the fourth year all its fruit shall be set aside for jubilation before the LORD,” and we expound on [the word] הלולים/jubilation like redeemed to become secular again, as the All Merciful said, redeem it and then eat it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:2)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים אֵין לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁטָּעוּן פִּדְיוֹן כְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי אֵין הַבְּעָלִים מוֹסִיפִים אֶת הַחֹמֶשׁ דְּלֹא כָּתְבָה תּוֹרָה בוֹ חֹמֶשׁ:


בית שמאי אומרים אין לו חומש – even though it requires redemption like the Second Tithe, the owners do not add the one-fifth for the Torah did not write [an added] fifth concerning it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:3)


**וְאֵין לוֹ בִעוּר.** אֵינוֹ חַיָּב לְבַעֲרוֹ מִן הַבַּיִת בְּעֶרֶב פֶּסַח שֶׁל רְבִיעִית וְשֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית כְּשֶׁמְּבַעֵר הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת כְּדִכְתִיב (דברים כו) בִּעַרְתִּי הַקֹּדֶשׁ מִן הַבַּיִת:


ואין לו ביעור – He is not obligated to remove it from the house on the Eve of Passover of the fourth year and the seventh year as one removes the tithes as it is written (Deuteronomy 26:13): “I have cleared out the consecrated portion from the house.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:4)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים יֶשׁ לוֹ.** חֹמֶשׁ וְיֶשׁ לוֹ בִעוּר. בֵּית הִלֵּל יַלְפֵי קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִמַּעֲשֵׂר, מַה מַּעֲשֵׂר יֵשׁ לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בִּעוּר, אַף כֶּרֶם רְבָעִי יֵשׁ לוֹ חֹמֶשׁ וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בִּעוּר, וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי לֹא יַלְפֵי קֹדֶשׁ קֹדֶשׁ מִמַּעֲשֵׂר:


וב"ה אומרים יש לו – [He has the added] fifth and he has removal. The School of Hillel derives [through a Gezerah Shavah/analogy] as he learns the fourth year fruit from the Second Tithe (Leviticus 19:24: “In the fourth year all its fruit shall be set aside (קדש) for jubilation before the LORD,” and Leviticus 27:30: “All tithes from the land, whether seed from the ground or fruit from the tree are the LORD’s; they are holy to the LORD.”). Just as the Second Tithe has the additional fifth and it has the removal from the house, so also the fourth-year fruit has the added firth and the it has removal from the house. But the School of Shammai does not derive “holy/holy” from the Second Tithe (see the above verses).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:5)


**יֶשׁ לוֹ פֶרֶט וְיֶשׁ לוֹ עוֹלְלוֹת.** דִּכְחֻלִּין חָשְׁבֵי לֵיהּ:


יש לו פרט ויש לו עוללות – they are considered like non-sacred/secular produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:6)


**וְהָעֲנִיִּים פּוֹדִין לְעַצְמָן.** מִן הַפֶּרֶט וְהָעוֹלְלוֹת שֶׁלָּקְטוּ, וְאוֹכְלִים אוֹתָן בִּמְקוֹמָן וּמַעֲלִין הַדָּמִים לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


והעניים פודין לעצמן – from the grapes fallen off during cutting/the poor man’s share and the gleaning reserved for the poor (i.e., the small single bunch on a single branch or hanging down directly from the trunk) that they had gleaned, eating them in their place and bringing their monetary value to Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:6:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים כֻּלּוֹ לַגָּת.** מִשּׁוּם דְּיַלְפֵי מִמַּעֲשֵׂר וְסָבְרֵי לְהוּ מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי מָמוֹן גָּבֹהַּ הוּא הִלְכָּךְ אֵין לָעֲנִיִּים חֵלֶק בּוֹ, וְדוֹרְכִים הָעוֹלְלוֹת עִם שְׁאָר הַיַּיִן וְהַבְּעָלִים מַעֲלִים הַכֹּל לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


ובית הלל אומרים כולו לגת – because they derive it from the Second Tithe and they hold that the Second Tithe is the money of the Most-High (God), therefore, the poor have no share in it. And they tread on the gleaning reserved for the poor with the rest of the wine and owners bring everything to Jerusalem.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:1)


**שֶׁכֻּלּוֹ עוֹלְלוֹת.** שֶׁאֵין בְּכָל הַכֶּרֶם אֶשְׁכּוֹל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ כָּתֵף וְנָטֵף:


שכולו עוללות – throughout the entire vineyard there is not a bunch of grapes that has grapes on an arm of the vine which branches off into twigs (כתף) and grapes hanging down directly from the trunk (נטף).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:2)


**אִם אֵין בָּצִיר.** וְכַמָּה שִׁעוּר בָּצִיר, שְׁלֹשָׁה אֶשְׁכּוֹלוֹת שֶׁעוֹשִׂים רְבִיעִית:


אם אין בציר – and what is the measure of a harvest? Three bunches which makes a fourth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:7:3)


**אָמַר לוֹ רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא וְכַרְמְךָ לֹא תְעוֹלֵל אֲפִלּוּ כֻלּוֹ עוֹלְלוֹת.** וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אָמַר דְּלֹא תֵימָא הוֹאִיל וְאֵין לָעֲנִיִּים בָּעוֹלְלוֹת קֹדֶם לַבָּצִיר יִזְכֶּה בָהֶם בַּעַל הַבַּיִת, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר וְכַרְמְךָ לֹא תְעוֹלֵל:


אמר לו רבי עקיבא וכרמך לא תעולל אפילו כולו עוללות – But Rabbi Eliezer said that you should say this since the poor don’t have [a share in] grapes hanging down directly from the trunk prior to the harvest, the owner of the house will get possession of them, and therefore it says (Leviticus 19:10): “You shall not pick your vineyard bare.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:1)


**מִשֶּׁנּוֹדְעוּ הָעוֹלְלוֹת.** שֶׁנִּכָּר מַה הוּא עוֹלֶלֶת וּמַה הוּא אֶשְׁכּוֹל:


משנודעו הועללות – when it is recognized what are grapes hanging down directly from the trunk and what is a bundle [of grapes].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:2)


**הָעוֹלְלוֹת לָעֲנִיִּים.** שֶׁאֵין אָדָם מַקְדִּישׁ דָּבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ שֶׁלּוֹ:


העוללות לעניים – A person cannot dedicate/sanctify [to the Temple] something that is not his.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:3)


**יִתְּנוּ שְׂכַר גִּדּוּלָן לַהֶקְדֵּשׁ.** מַה שֶּׁמַּשְׁבִּיחִין כָּל שָׁעָה בְּתוֹךְ קַרְקַע שֶׁל הֶקְדֵּשׁ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


יתנו שכר גדולן להקדש – that which grows in value all the while that they are in the ground of something dedicated to the Temple. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:4)


**בֶּעָרִיס.** כֶּרֶם שֶׁהַגְּפָנִים שֶׁלָּהּ מוּדְלוֹת עַל גַּבֵּי כְּלוּנְסוֹת וְעֵצִים, לְשׁוֹן אַף עַרְשֵׂנוּ רַעֲנָנָה (שיר השירים א):


בעריס – a vineyards where its vines hang on poles and trees, based upon the language (Song of Songs 1:16): “Our couch is in a bower.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:5)


**כָּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִפְשֹׁט.** כְּלוֹמַר לְאַחַר שֶׁעָבַר מֵעָלֶיהָ, אִם בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁנִּזְכַּר אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לִפְשֹׁט יָדוֹ וְלִטֹּל קָרֵינָא בֵיהּ לֹא תָשׁוּב לְקַחְתּוֹ (דברים כד):


כל שאינו יכול לפשוט – that is to say, after he has passed by them, and if in the place where he remembers he cannot stretch his hand and take, it is called concerning him, (Deuteronomy 24:19): “Do not turn back to get it.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:6)


**אֲבָל רוֹגְלִיּוֹת.** הַגְּפָנִים הָעוֹמְדִים עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע שֶׁדּוֹרְכִים עֲלֵיהֶם בָּרֶגֶל:


אבל רוגליות –[grapes growing in a row on isolated vines] grapes that stand on the ground when they tread upon them with the foot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 7:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/7:8:7)


**מִשֶּׁיַּעֲבֹר מִמֶּנָּה.** הֲוֵי שִׁכְחָה, דְּכָל גֶּפֶן וָגֶפֶן מִן הָרוֹגְלִיּוֹת חֲשִׁיבָא כְּאֻמָּן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, וְאָסוּר לָשׁוּב מֵאֻמָּן לְאֻמָּן:


משיעבור ממנה – that is forgetting, for each and every vine among the grapes growing in a row on isolated vines is considered like a border-bed/furrow on its own, and it is prohibited to return from one border bed to another border bed.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:1)


**מֵאֵימָתַי הַנְּמוֹשׁוֹת.** אִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי זְקֵנִים הַהוֹלְכִים עַל מִשְׁעַנְתָּם. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי לִקּוּטֵי בָתַר לִקּוּטֵי. שֶׁמֵּאַחַר שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ בַּשָּׂדֶה לוֹקְטִים אַחַר לוֹקְטִים אוֹ הַזְּקֵנִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁמְּאַחֲרִים לָלֶכֶת לְלַקֵּט אַחַר הַכֹּל כְּבָר נִתְיָאֲשׁוּ הָעֲנִיִּים מֵאוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה, וְכָל הַנִּמְצָא בָהּ אַחַר כָּךְ הוּא הֶפְקֵר לַכֹּל, בֵּין לַעֲנִיִּים בֵּין לַעֲשִׁירִים:


מאימתי. הנמושות – there are those who explain this [word נמושות/the last troop of gleaners ] as old people who are walking on their staffs, and there are those who interpret this as the gleaners after the gleaners (the poor who come for the second gleanings – see Talmud Bava Metzia 21b) , for after they would go in the field, gleaners after the gleaners or these elderly people who come late to walk go to glean after everyone that is poor has already despaired from that field, and all that is found in it after that is ownerless to all, whether for the poor or for the rich.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:2)


**רְבִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה.** הַמָּטָר נִקְרָא רְבִיעָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא רוֹבֵעַ אֶת הָאָרֶץ וּמוֹלִידָהּ וּמַצְמִיחָהּ כְּזָכָר הָרוֹבֵעַ אֶת הַנְּקֵבָה וְהִיא מִתְעַבֶּרֶת מִמֶּנּוּ, לְשׁוֹן בְּהֶמְתְּךָ לֹא תַרְבִּיעַ (ויקרא יט). פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר רְבִיעָה עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַמָּטָר מַשְׁכִּיב הֶעָפָר וּמַרְבִּיצוֹ, תַּרְגּוּם רוֹבֵץ רְבִיעַ. וּזְמַן רְבִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה בְּשָׁנָה בֵּינוֹנִית הוּא בְּכ"ג בְּמַרְחֶשְׁוָן:


רביעה שניה – the rain is called fructification (in the fall) because it fructifies the ground (see Talmud Ta’anit 6a), and gives birth and causes to grow like a male who copulates with a female and she becomes pregnant from him, from the language (Leviticus 19:19): “You shall not let cattle mate [with a different kind]…” Another explanation: Fructification on account that the rain causes the dust to lie down, which is the Aramaic translation of lying down to cause fructification. And he time for the second rainfall in an average year is on the twenty-third of Mar Heshvan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:3)


**שֶׁאֵינָן מוֹסְקִין.** מְסִיקָה בְּזֵיתִים כְּמוֹ קוֹצֵר בִּתְבוּאָה, בּוֹצֵר בַּעֲנָבִים, אוֹרֶה בִּתְאֵנִים, גּוֹדֵר בִּתְמָרִים, כָּךְ מוֹסֵק בְּזֵיתִים:


שאינן מוסקין – harvesting of olives like reaping grain and cutting grapes and plucking figs and harvesting dates, so too harvesting olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:1:4)


**וְלֹא יְהֵא מֵבִיא בְּאַרְבָּעָה אִסָּרוֹת.** דְּהַיְנוּ שְׁנֵי פוּנְדְיוֹנִים, שֶׁהַפּוּנְדְיוֹן שְׁנֵי אִסָּרִין, וּתְנַן אֵין פּוֹחֲתִין לֶעָנִי הָעוֹבֵר מִמָּקוֹם לְמָקוֹם מִכִּכָּר בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן, דְּהַיְנוּ מְזוֹן שְׁתֵּי סְעֻדּוֹת, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵינוֹ מֵבִיא זֵיתִים שָׁוֶה אַרְבָּעָה אִסָּרוֹת, דְּהַיְנוּ מְזוֹן שְׁתֵּי סְעֻדּוֹת לוֹ וּשְׁתֵּי סְעֻדּוֹת לְאִשְׁתּוֹ, אַף הוּא אֵינוֹ יוֹצֵא לִלְקֹט. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ולא יהא מביא בארבעה איסרות – that is two Pundiyons/Dupondium (a Roman coin equal to two Asses) as a Pundiyon is two Issars, and we teach in the Mishnah that a poor person that goes from place to place from the [town] square receives not less that a Pundiyon/Dupondium, that is the food for two meals, and since he does not bring olives worth four Issars, that is the food for two meals for himself and two meals for his wife, for even he does not go out to glean. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:1)


**נֶאֱמָנִים.** הָעֲנִיִּים לוֹמַר חִטִּים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁל לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה הֵן, וּפְטוּרִים מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר:


נאמנים – the poor [are believed] to state that this wheat is of gleanings, forgotten sheaves and the corner of the field, and they are exempt from tithing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:2)


**בִּשְׁעָתָן.** בִּשְׁעַת הַקָּצִיר:


בשעתן – at the time of the harvest.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:3)


**בְּכָל שְׁנָתוֹ.** בְּשָׁנָה שְׁלִישִׁית וּבְשָׁנָה שִׁשִּׁית, שֶׁמַּעְשַׂר עָנִי נוֹהֵג בָּהֶן:


בכל שנתו – in the third year and in the sixth year where the Poor Tithe applies to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:4)


**וּבֶן לֵוִי נֶאֱמָן לְעוֹלָם.** דְּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן נוֹהֵג בְּכָל שָׁנָה, וְכֵיוָן דְּאָמַר שֶׁל מַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן הֵם לֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא לֹא הִפְרִישׁ מֵהֶן תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר, דְּכִי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל תְּרוּמָה גְדוֹלָה, כָּךְ לֹא נֶחְשַׁד בֶּן לֵוִי עַל תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר:


ובן לוי נאמן לעולם – since the First Tithe applies in each year, and since that he said that they are First Tithe, we do not suspect lest he did not separate from them the Tithe of the Tithe, and just as an Israelite was not suspected regarding the Large Terumah (i.e., two percent to the Kohen), the Levite was not suspect on the Tithe of the Tithe (that was given by a Levite to a Kohen).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:2:5)


**אֶלָּא עַל דָּבָר שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם נוֹהֲגִין כֵּן.** נוֹהֲגִים לָתֵת לָהֶם כֵּן, וְלֹא עַל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין רְגִילִים לָתֵת לָהֶם כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ, וּלְקַמָּן מְפָרֵשׁ וְאָזֵיל:


אלא על דבר שבני אדם נוהגין כן – It was practice to give them this and not on anything that they were not accustomed to give them similar to this, and further on, it explains this.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:1)


**נֶאֱמָנִים עַל הַחִטִּים.** לוֹמַר שֶׁל מַעְשַׂר עָנִי הֵם שֶׁנִּתַּן לִי, אֲבָל אֵין נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר קֶמַח וּפַת זֶה שֶׁל מַעְשַׂר עָנִי, שֶׁנִּתַּן לִי קֶמַח וּפַת, דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְחַלֵּק מַעְשַׂר עָנִי קֶמַח וּפַת:


נאמנים על החיטים – [they are believed] to say that they are of the Poor Man’s tithe that was given to me, but they are not believed to say that flour and this bread is of the Poor Man’s tithe, that flour and bread were given to me, for it is not the manner to distribute [processed] Poor Man’s tithe of flour and bread.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:2)


**שְׂעוֹרָה שֶׁל אֹרֶז.** אִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי שִׁבֹּלֶת שֶׁל אֹרֶז, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה דַרְכָּם לְחַלֵּק אֹרֶז שֶׁל מַעְשַׂר עָנִי אֶלָּא בְּשִׁבֳּלִים. וְאִית דִּמְפָרְשֵׁי שְׂעוֹרָה שֶׁל אֹרֶז קֹדֶם שֶׁנִּכְתַּשׁ בַּמַּכְתֵּשׁ וְהוּסְרָה קְלִפָּתוֹ [שְׂעוֹרָה קוֹרֵא] לוֹ, וּלְאַחַר דִּישָׁה בְּעוֹדוֹ בִקְלִפָּתוֹ דֶּרֶךְ לְחַלְּקוֹ לָעֲנִיִּים:


שעורה של אורז – there are those who explain spike of rice, for it was not their manner to distributer rice of the Poor Man’s Tithe other than spike/ears of corn. And there are those who explain barley of of rice prior to its being crushed in a mortar and the remove of its shell [it is called barley] and after threshing while its hide is on it, it is the practice to distribute it to the poor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:3)


**גְּרִיסִין.** שֶׁטְּחָנָן בְּרֵיחַיִם שֶׁל גָּרוֹסוֹת:


גריסין – that they are ground in a millstone of grist-grinder’s mills.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:4)


**נֶאֱמָנִים עַל הַשֶּׁמֶן.** שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ לְחַלֵּק מַעְשַׂר עָנִי בְּשֶׁמֶן:


נאמנים על השמן – it is the manner to distribute the Poor Man’s Tithe in oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:3:5)


**וְאֵין נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר שֶׁל זֵיתֵי נִקּוּף הוּא.** זֵיתֵי נִקּוּף מִמַּתְּנוֹת עֲנִיִּים הֵן, שֶׁחוֹבְטִים וּמְנַקְּפִים בַּזַּיִת לְהַשִּׁיר הַזֵּיתִים שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ מִן הַמְּסִיקָה. וְנִקּוּף לְשׁוֹן כְּנוֹקֵף זַיִת. וְאֵין הֶעָנִי נֶאֱמָן לוֹמַר שֶׁמֶן זֶה הוֹצֵאתִי מִזֵּיתֵי נִקּוּף וּפָטוּר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין רְגִילִין לַעֲשׂוֹת שֶׁמֶן מִזֵּיתֵי נִקּוּף. אֲבָל עָנִי הָאוֹמֵר קֶמַח זֶה אוֹ פַת זוֹ מִלֶּקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה שֶׁלָּקַטְתִּי וְטָחַנְתִּי קֶמַח וְאָפִיתִי לֶחֶם, נֶאֱמָן, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הֶעָנִי לַעֲשׂוֹת לֶחֶם מִלֶּקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה:


ואין נאמנים לומר של זיתי נקוף הוא – the olives left on the tree for the poor/the gleanings are from the gifts of the poor, that we beat and knock the olive to empty the olives that remained from the harvesting. And this striking is the language like pounding an olive. But the poor person is not believed to state that this oil I removed from pounded olives and he is exempt from tithes, because it is not customary to make oil from pounded olives. But the poor person who states that this flour or bread is from the gleanings, forgotten sheaf and corner of the field that I gleaned and that I grinded the flour and baked the bread, is believed for it is the manner of the poor person to make bread from gleanings, forgotten sheaves and the corner of the field.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:1)


**נֶאֱמָנִים.** הָעֲנִיִּים עַל הַיָּרָק חַי לוֹמַר שֶׁל מַעְשַׂר עָנִי הוּא, דְּיָרָק חַיָּב בְּמַעֲשֵׂר מִדְּרַבָּנָן:


נאמנים – the poor people [are believed] about raw vegetables to state that they are from the Poor Man’s Tithe, for vegetables are liable for tithes from the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:2)


**דָּבָר מֻעָט.** נֶאֱמָנִים אַף עַל הַמְּבֻשָּׁל, דִּפְעָמִים שֶׁבַּעַל הַבַּיִת שָׁכַח מִלְּעַשֵּׂר וּמְעַשֵּׂר מִן הַמְּבֻשָּׁל מִתּוֹךְ הַקְּדֵרָה:


דבר מועט – they are believed even on that which is cooked, for sometimes the owner of the house forgot to tithe and tithes from that which is cooked from within the pot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:4:3)


**מִלְּפָסוֹ.** הַקְּדֵרָה אוֹ הַפָּרוּר שֶׁמְּבַשֵּׁל בָּהּ:


מלפסו – the pot/dish or round pot that he is cooking in.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:1)


**אֵין פּוֹחֲתִין לָעֲנִיִּים בַּגֹּרֶן.** כְּשֶׁמְּחַלְּקִין מַעְשַׂר עָנִי בַּגֹּרֶן אֵין נוֹתְנִין לְכָל עָנִי וְעָנִי פָּחוֹת מִשִּׁעוּר זֶה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כו) וְאָכְלוּ בִשְׁעָרֶיךָ וְשָׂבֵעוּ, תֵּן לוֹ כְּדֵי שָׂבְעוֹ:


אין פוחתין לעניים בגורן – when they distribute the Poor Man’s Tithe in the granary/threshing floor, they don’t give to each and every poor person less than this measure, as it is written (Deuteronomy 26:12): “that they may eat their full in your settlements,” give him according to his fullness/satisfaction.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:2)


**דְּבֵלָה.** תְּאֵנִים יְבֵשִׁים לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּדְרָסִים בָּעִגּוּל קְרוּיִין דְּבֵלָה, וְשׁוּב אֵין נִמְכָּרִים בְּמִדָּה אֶלָּא בְמִשְׁקָל, לְהָכִי תָּנֵי מָנֶה דְבֵלָה, וּמָנֶה הוּא מִשְׁקַל מֵאָה דִּינָרִים וְהַדִּינָר מִשְׁקַל שִׁשָּׁה מָעִים וּמִשְׁקַל הַמָּעָה שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר גַּרְגְּרֵי שְׂעוֹרָה:


דבלה – dry figs after they had been tread in a round mould [in which figs are pressed] which is called cake of pressed figs. And further, they are not sold by measurement but rather by weight, therefore it teaches [in the Mishnah] a Maneh of a cake of pressed figs, and a Maneh is a weight of one hundred Denars and a Denar is a weight of six Ma’ah and the weight of a M’ah is sixteen berries/grains of barley.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:5:3)


**פְּרָס.** חֲצִי מָנֶה, וּבְכָל הַמִּדּוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בְּמַתְנִיתִין הֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא וַהֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל. וְלֹא נֶאֶמְרוּ דְבָרִים הַלָּלוּ אֶלָּא לַמְחַלֵּק מַעְשַׂר עָנִי בַּגֹּרֶן, אֲבָל הַמְחַלֵּק מַעְשַׂר עָנִי בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתוֹ מְחַלֵּק כְּפִי רְצוֹנוֹ, וְלֹא נָתְנוּ בוֹ חֲכָמִים שִׁעוּר:


פרס – one-half of a Maneh, and in all of the measurements that are stated in our Mishnah, the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher and the Halakha is according to Abba Shaul. And these things are not said other than to distribute the Poor Man’s Tithe in the threshing floor/granary, butr he who distributes the Poor Man’s Tithe in his home, distributes according to his desires and the Sages did not give him a measurement.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:1)


**מִדָּה זוֹ.** דְּאֵין פּוֹחֲתִין לֶעָנִי אֲמוּרָה בְּכֹהֲנִים לְוִיִּם וְיִשְׂרְאֵלִים, כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם שֶׁמְּחַלֵּק מַעְשַׂר עָנִי בַּגֹּרֶן לֹא יִפְחֹת מִזֶּה הַשִּׁעוּר:


מדה זו – since we don’t give any less to the poor that is stated for Kohanim, Levites and Israelites, each one of them to whom we distribute the Poor Man’s Tithe in the threshing floor/granary should not receive less than this measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:2)


**הָיָה מַצִּיל.** שֶׁאֵין רוֹצֶה לְחַלֵּק לָעֲנִיִּים שֶׁבָּאוּ כָּל מַעְשַׂר עָנִי שֶׁבְּיָדוֹ, וְרוֹצֶה לְהַצִּיל מִמֶּנּוּ לִקְרוֹבָיו הָעֲנִיִּים:


היה מציל – for he does not want to distribute to the poor who came all the Poor Man’s Tithe that is in his hand, but he wants to save from it for his poor relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:3)


**נוֹטֵל מֶחֱצָה.** וּמַצְנִיעַ לְצֹרֶךְ קְרוֹבָיו:


נוטל מחצה – and hides it for the needs of his relatives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:6:4)


**וְנוֹתֵן מֶחֱצָה.** לָעֲנִיִּים שֶׁבָּאוּ. וְאִם לְאַחַר שֶׁנָּטַל מֶחֱצָה וְהִצְנִיעַ לְצֹרֶךְ קְרוֹבָיו הָיָה לוֹ דָּבָר מֻעָט, כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא נִשְׁאַר לוֹ כְּדֵי לָתֵת לְכָל אֶחָד מִן הָעֲנִיִּים שֶׁבָּאוּ כַּשִּׁעוּר הַקָּצוּב לְעֵיל בְּמַתְנִיתִין, נוֹתֵן לִפְנֵיהֶם מַה שֶּׁנִּשְׁאַר בְּיָדוֹ וְהֵן מְחַלְּקִים בֵּינֵיהֶם:


ונותן מחצה – to the poor people came, and if after he took the half and hid it for the needs of his relatives, he had a little bit, that is to say, that thre didn’t remain to him enough to give to every one of the poor people according to the fixed measurement mentioned above in our Mishnah, he places it before them what remains in his hand in order that they can divide it among themselves.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:1)


**מִכִּכָּר בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן.** כִּכָּר הַנִּמְכָּר בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן כְּשֶׁהַחִטִּים נִמְכָּרִים אַרְבַּע סְאִים לְסֶלַע. וְאַרְבַּע סְאִין הֵן עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה קַבִּין, שֶׁכָּל סְאָה הִיא שִׁשָּׁה קַבִּין, וְהַסֶּלַע הוּא אַרְבָּעָה דִינָרִים וְכָל דִּינָר שִׁשָּׁה מָעִין, נִמְצָא הַסֶּלַע עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מָעִין, נִמְצְאוּ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה קַבִּין לְעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה מָעִין, קַב לְכָל מָעָה, וְהַמָּעָה שְׁנֵי פוּנְדְיוֹנִים, הֲרֵי הַכִּכָּר הַנִּמְכָּר בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן הוּא חֲצִי קַב. אֲבָל מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַחֶנְוָנִי רוֹצֶה לְהִשְׂתַּכֵּר לִיצִיאַת הָאֲפִיָּה וְהַטְּחִינָה אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁיִּמָּכֵר הַכִּכָּר שֶׁהוּא חֲצִי קַב בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן כְּפִי הָעֵרֶךְ שֶׁמּוֹכְרִים הַתְּבוּאָה בַּשּׁוּק אַרְבַּע סְאִים בְּסֶלַע, לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יִהְיֶה הַכִּכָּר הַנִּמְכָּר בְּפוּנְדְיוֹן אֶלָּא רֹבַע הַקַּב בִּלְבָד. וּכְשֶׁמְּחַלְּקִים תְּבוּאָה לֶעָנִי בַּגֹּרֶן וְעָלָיו לִטְחֹן וְלֶאֱפוֹת אֵין פּוֹחֲתִין מֵחֲצִי קַב, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁנּוֹתְנִים לוֹ כִּכָּר אָפוּי לֹא יִהְיֶה אֶלָּא רֹבַע הַקַּב שֶׁהֵם שִׁשָּׁה בֵּיצִים. וְהָכִי מְפֹרָשׁ בְּפֶרֶק כֵּיצַד מִשְׁתַּתְּפִין בְּעֵרוּבִין (דף פב):


ככר בפונדיון – bread that is sold for a Pundiyon when the wheat is sold at four S’eah for a Selah, and these four Se’ah are twenty-four Kabs, and each Se’ah is six Kabs and the Selah is four Denars and each Denar is six Ma’ahs , it is found that the Selah is twenty-four Ma’ah and and it is found that twenty-four kabs for twenty-four Ma’ah, a Kab for each Ma’ah. But because the storekeeper wants to make money for his expenditures of baking and grinding, it is impossible to sell the bread which is one-half Kab per Pundiyon according to the value that they sell grain in the market place, four Se’ah for a Selah, therefore, the bread is not sold for a Pundiyon but only for one-quarter Kab. And when they distribute the grain to the poor in the grainary and it is upon him to grind [it] and bake [it], they don’t give less than one-half Kab, but when they give him a baked loaf, he does not get other than one-quarter of a Kab which is six eggs. This is explained in the [eighth] chapter of Tractate Eruvin, the Chapter “How do you prepare a Shittuf for the Sabbath line?”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:2)


**פַּרְנָסַת לִינָה.** מִטָּה וְכָרִים וּכְסָתוֹת:


פרנסת לינה – a bed, pillows and cushions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:3)


**מְזוֹן שָׁלֹשׁ סְעֻדּוֹת.** שֶׁחַיָּב אָדָם לֶאֱכֹל בַּשַּׁבָּת שָׁלֹשׁ סְעֻדּוֹת:


מזון שלש סעודות – for a person is liable to eat three meals on the Sabbath.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:4)


**לֹא יִטֹּל מִן הַתַּמְחוּי.** לְפִי שֶׁהַתַּמְחוּי מִתְחַלֵּק בְּכָל יוֹם, שֶׁהַגַּבָּאִים מַחֲזִירִים עַל פִּתְחֵי בַּעֲלֵי בָתִּים בְּכָל יוֹם וְלוֹקְחִים מֵהֶם תַּבְשִׁיל לְצֹרֶךְ הָעֲנִיִּים, וְהַכְּלִי שֶׁמְּשִׂימִים בּוֹ אֶת הַתַּבְשִׁיל נִקְרָא תַּמְחוּי:


לא יטול מן התמחוי – because the soup-kitchen is distributed every day and the treasurers/managers go back out to the openings of the home owners on each day and take from them food for the needs of the poor, and the utensil that they place the food is called a תמחוי/ charity plate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:5)


**לֹא יִטֹּל מִן הַקֻּפָּה.** לְפִי שֶׁהַקֻּפָּה מִתְחַלֶּקֶת מֵעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת לְעֶרֶב שַׁבָּת:


לא יטול מן הקופה – for the communal fund is distributed from Friday to Friday.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:6)


**נִגְבֵּית בִּשְׁנַיִם.** לְפִי שֶׁמְּמַשְׁכְּנִים עַל הַצְּדָקָה, וְאֵין עוֹשִׂים שְׂרָרָה עַל הַצִּבּוּר פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁנַיִם:


נגבית בשנים – because they may [forcibly] take collateral, and one doesn’t make an authority over a community with less than two [people].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:7:7)


**וּמִתְחַלֶּקֶת בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה.** לְפִי שֶׁהִיא כְּדִינֵי מָמוֹנוֹת וְדִינֵי מָמוֹנוֹת בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה:


ומתחלקת בשלשה – because it is like the monetary matters and monetary matters are with three (see the beginning of Tractate Sanhedrin).



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:1)


**מָאתַיִם זוּז.** קִים לְהוּ לְרַבָּנָן שֶׁאֵלּוּ מַסְפִּיקִים לְשָׁנָה אַחַת לִכְסוּת וְלִמְזוֹנוֹת:


מאתים זוז – the Rabbis have established that [two hundred zuz] is sufficient for one year for clothing and food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:2)


**אוֹ לִכְתֻבַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא יוֹשֶׁבֶת תַּחְתָּיו:


או לכתובת אשתו – and even if she is dwelling beneath him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:8:3)


**וְאֶת כְּלֵי תַשְׁמִישׁוֹ.** כֵּלִים יָפִים שֶׁמִּשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶם בַּשַּׁבָּתוֹת וּבָרְגָלִים. וְהַנֵּי מִילֵי כְּשֶׁבָּא לִטֹּל לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל מִקֻּפָּה שֶׁל צְדָקָה, שֶׁמִּתְפַּרְנֵס בְּצִנְעָה וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל מִמַּה שֶּׁבְּיַד גַּבַּאי, אֲבָל אִם נוֹטֵל מִקֻּפָּה שֶׁל צְדָקָה אָז אֵין מַנִּיחִין אוֹתוֹ לִטֹּל אֲפִלּוּ לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה עַד שֶׁיִּמְכֹּר כְּלֵי תַשְׁמִישׁוֹ:


ואת כלי תשמישו – nice utensils that he makes use of on Shabbat and Festivals, and these words when he comes to take gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner of the field and he doesn’t take from the charitable communal fund that he can support himself in private, and he doesn’t take from is in the hand of the treasurer/manager, but if he takes from the charitable communal fund then we don’t let him to take even gleanings, the forgotten sheaf or the corner of the field until he sells his tools/dishes.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:9:1)


**מִי שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים זוּז וְכוּ'.** דַּחֲמִשִּׁין דְּעָבְדִין טָבִין כְּמָאתַיִם דְּלֹא עָבְדִין:


מי שיש לו חמשים זוז וכו' – for fifty [zuz] for good service is like two-hundred for one who does not work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Peah 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Peah/r/8:9:2)


**וְכָל מִי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִטֹּל וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל וְכוּ'.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁדּוֹחֵק עַצְמוֹ בִּמְלָאכָה וּמִסְתַּפֵּק בְּמַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִתְפַּרְנֵס מֵאֲחֵרִים. אֲבָל אִם אֵין מַעֲשֵׂה יָדָיו מַסְפִּיקִין לוֹ וּמְסַגֵּף עַצְמוֹ בְּחַיֵּי צַעַר קְרוֹבִין לְמִיתָה, עַל זֶה אָמְרוּ כָּל הַצָּרִיךְ לִטֹּל וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטֵל הֲרֵי זֶה שׁוֹפֵךְ דָּמִים, וְאָסוּר לְרַחֵם עָלָיו, עַל נַפְשֵׁיהּ לֹא חַיֵּס כָּל שֶׁכֵּן עַל אֲחֵרִים:


וכל מי שצריך ליטול ואינו נוטל וכו' – such as case where he pushes himself in his work and suffices with the work of his hands in order that he doesn’t have to be supported from others, but if the work of his hands is not sufficient for him and he afflicts himself in a life of pain that near death, on this they said, that whomever needs to take and does not take, he is like one who sheds blood and it is forbidden to have mercy upon him, for he does not have consideration for his own soul, how much more so upon the souls of others.
